# 通用工具调用型 Agent 任务

请依据当前运行包 `run_manifest.json` 指向的三张保持原始基表结构的答案隔离
工作簿和 `inputs/T_CSES_144-2024.pdf`，从全部基102设备候选中自主完成固定燃烧源工业锅炉或
火电热力排放清单编制。你可以使用通用文件、终端、代码与表格工具，但
不得使用 fixed-combustion-inventory 专用工具、专用 Skills、冻结规则包、
输入适配配置、其他方法输出、参照包或遗留结果公式。不得浏览当前运行包
以外的工程目录；当前运行包以外发现的任何代码、规则、结果都不属于本次
可用输入。

输入工作簿保留基102、基101企业及治理设施表的原始行粒度；污染物历史产生量和
排放量已统一留空。只能根据未留空的源字段和 PDF 自主建立处理逻辑，不得把空白结果列
解释为零，也不得从其他位置寻找旧答案。必须对运行包内全部候选记录作出
工业锅炉、火电热力或范围外判断，再仅汇总 `run_manifest.json` 指定目标类型。
`inputs/source_identity_index.json` 只提供目标中立的候选 `source_id` 与基102行对应关系，
可用于稳定标识；它不包含源类答案，禁止根据ID格式推断去向。

输出必须写入当前运行包的 `outputs` 目录，至少生成以下三个 UTF-8 文件：

1. `source_decisions.csv`：每个候选源一行，必须包含
   `source_id,requested_target,decided_target,disposition,reason_code`。
2. `generic_inventory_items.csv`：每个源×污染物一行，必须包含
   `source_id,target,pollutant,status,method,activity_record_json,parameter_record_json,control_record_json,generation_t,emission_t,standard_reference,reason_code`。

   三个 `*_record_json` 字段必须是合法 JSON 数组，不得写成单个数值或
   JSON 对象。每一个燃料或独立计算组件在三个数组中各占一个元素，
   数组长度、元素顺序及 `component_id` 必须一一对应。多燃料不得先汇总
   活动水平或排放因子后再记录。元素的最低字段如下：

   - `activity_record_json`：`component_id,value,unit`；
   - `parameter_record_json`：`component_id,parameter_id,mode,value,unit`；
   - `control_record_json`：`component_id,method,efficiency,fine_efficiency,coarse_efficiency`。

   对 SO₂、NOx、CO、VOC、PM₂.₅、BC、OC 和 NH₃，`efficiency` 记录该组件
   实际使用的去除效率，无适用治理时明确写 `0`。对 PM₁₀，
   `fine_efficiency` 和 `coarse_efficiency` 分别记录 PM₂.₅ 组分与
   PM₂.₅–₁₀ 组分的去除效率，两者均必须给出；不得只写一个
   `control_efficiency`。例如两种燃料时，三个数组必须均有2个元素，
   分别以相同的 `component_id` 对齐。
3. `generic_source_disposition.csv`：每个候选源记录一行，必须包含
   `source_id,target,disposition,reason_code`。
4. `generic_exception_list.csv`：每个需要用户处理的根因组一行，必须包含
   `exception_id,source_id,pollutant,root_cause,action_required`。
5. `generic_run_summary.json`：记录输入文件、处理源数、核算项数、各状态数、
   输出路径、未完成事项和你实际采用的规则/脚本文件路径。

`status` 只允许使用：`calculated`、`not_involved`、`information_insufficient`、
`source_data_invalid`。数值为空必须给出状态和 `reason_code`；信息不足时不得
编造燃料量、因子、治理效率或排放量。完成后检查四个 CSV 的列名、唯一性、
数值非负性和范围覆盖，再报告实际输出路径和未完成事项。

排放空间分配方法\n排放空间分配方法是指生成排放空间栅格以满足空气质量模拟的平需求。排放空间分配尽可能在第\n四级排放源层面按照点源和面源分别处理完成，点源和面源排放空间分配方法如下：\na) 点源具有明确位置标识，根据其经纬度坐标将点源排放直接定位在网格，活动水平调查时收\n集排污设备经纬度。\nb) {\\\"CO\\\": \\\"CO\\\", \\\"NOx\\\": \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"SO2\\\": \\\"SO2\\\", \\\"VOC\\\": \\\"VOCs\\\",\\n          \\\"PM2.5\\\": \\\"PM25\\\", \\\"PM10\\\": \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\": \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\": \\\"OC\\\"}\\n\\ndef component_factors(sid, comp, fuel_type, amount, unit, sector, row):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"返回 (factors, params, problems)\\n       factors: {pollutant: (ef_value, mode, unit)} 仅含可计算的污染物\\n       params: 参数记录列表\\n       problems: [(pollutant, reason)] 无法计算的原因\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    factors, params, problems = {}, [], []\\n    d1_fuel = FUEL_MAP.get(fuel_type, None)\\n    if d1_fuel is None:\\n        for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"CO\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]:\\n            problems.append((p, \\\"fuel_factor_unavailable_in_table_d1\\\"))\\n        return factors, params, problems\\n\\n    # 燃烧技术确定\\n    if d1_fuel in COAL_FUELS:\\n        tech_raw = row.get(\\\"电站锅炉燃烧方式\\\") if pd.notna(row.get(\\\"电站锅炉燃烧方式\\\")) else None\\n        tech = TECH_MAP.get(str(tech_raw)) if tech_raw is not None else None\\n    elif d1_fuel in GAS_FUELS:\\n        tech = \\\"燃气锅炉\\\"\\n    else:\\n        tech = \\\"燃油锅炉\\\"\\n\\n    if tech is None:\\n        for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"CO\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]:\\n            problems.append((p, \\\"combustion_method_unavailable\\\"))\\n        return factors, params, problems\\n\\n    d1 = d1_lookup(sector, d1_fuel, tech)\\n    if d1 is None:\\n        for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"CO\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]:\\n            problems.append((p, \\\"no_d1_row_for_fuel_tech\\\"))\\n        return factors, params, problems\\n\\n    is_gas = d1_fuel in GAS_FUELS and tech == \\\"燃气锅炉\\\"\\n    ef_unit = \\\"g/m3\\\" if is_gas else \\\"g/kg\\\"\\n\\n    # 煤炭类物料衡算参数\\n    sr = ar = fpm25 = fpm10 = fbc = foc = None\\n    if d1_fuel in COAL_FUELS:\\n        c1 = c_lookup(TABLE_C1, sector, tech)\\n        c2 = c_lookup(TABLE_C2, sector, tech)\\n        c3 = c_lookup(TABLE_C3, sector, tech)\\n        if c1 is None or c2 is None or c3 is None:\\n            for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"CO\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]:\\n                problems.append((p, \\\"coal_param_row_unavailable\\\"))\\n            return factors, params, problems\\n        sr, ar = float(c1[\\\"sr\\\"]), float(c1[\\\"ar\\\"])\\n        fpm25, fpm10 = float(c2[\\\"fpm25\\\"]), float(c2[\\\"fpm10\\\"])\\n        fbc, foc = float(c3[\\\"fbc\\\"]), float(c3[\\\"foc\\\"])\\n\\n    # 含硫量 / 灰分 (燃料X平均收到基)\\n    s_val = row.get(\\\"燃料一平均收到基含硫量\\\") if comp == \\\"F1\\\" else row.get(\\\"燃料二平均收到基含硫量\\\")\\n    s_unit = row.get(\\\"燃料一平均收到基含硫量单位\\\") if comp == \\\"F1\\\" else row.get(\\\"燃料二平均收到基含硫量单位\\\")\\n    ash_val = row.get(\\\"燃料一平均收到基灰分（%）\\\") if comp == \\\"F1\\\" else row.get(\\\"燃料二平均收到基灰分（%）\\\")\\n    s_num = float(s_val) if pd.notna(s_val) else None\\n    ash_num = float(ash_val) if pd.notna(ash_val) else None\\n\\n    # ---- 各污染物产生系数 ----\\n    # SO2\\n    if d1[\\\"SO2\\\"] == \\\"物料衡算\\\":\\n        if s_num is None or (s_unit is not None and \\\"%\\\" not in str(s_unit)):\\n            problems.append((\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"sulfur_content_missing_or_unit_unexpected\\\"))\\n        else:\\n            ef = 20.0 * s_num * (1.0 - sr)   # 公式(3): 2*S(g/kg)*(1-sr), S(%)=10*g/kg\\n            factors[\\\"SO2\\\"] = (ef, \\\"material_balance_formula3\\\", ef_unit)\\n            params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"S_content\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"reported_input\\\", \\\"value\\\": s_num, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"%\\\"})\\n            params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"sr_bottom_ash_s_ratio\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C1_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": sr, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n    else:\\n        factors[\\\"SO2\\\"] = (float(d1[\\\"SO2\\\"]), \\\"table_D1_default\\\", ef_unit)\\n    # NOx\\n    if d1[\\\"NOx\\\"] == \\\"装机容量b\\\":\\n        cap = row.get(\\\"对应机组装机容量（万千瓦）\\\")\\n        if pd.notna(cap):\\n            ef = nox_capacity_factor(float(cap))\\n            factors[\\\"NOx\\\"] = (ef, \\\"table_D1_capacity_note_b\\\", ef_unit)\\n            params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"unit_capacity\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"reported_input\\\", \\\"value\\\": float(cap), \\\"unit\\\": \\\"万千瓦\\\"})\\n        else:\\n            problems.append((\\\"NOx\\\", \\\"capacity_missing_for_nox_factor\\\"))\\n    else:\\n        factors[\\\"NOx\\\"] = (float(d1[\\\"NOx\\\"]), \\\"table_D1_default\\\", ef_unit)\\n    # CO / VOC\\n    factors[\\\"CO\\\"] = (float(d1[\\\"CO\\\"]), \\\"table_D1_default\\\", ef_unit)\\n    factors[\\\"VOC\\\"] = (float(d1[\\\"VOCs\\\"]), \\\"table_D1_default\\\", ef_unit)\\n    # PM2.5 / PM10 / BC / OC\\n    for p, col in [(\\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM25\\\"), (\\\"PM10\\\", \\\"PM10\\\")]:\\n        if d1[col] == \\\"物料衡算\\\":\\n            if ash_num is None:\\n                problems.append((p, \\\"ash_content_missing\\\"))\\n            else:\\n                ef = 10.0 * ash_num * (1.0 - ar) * (fpm25 if p == \\\"PM2.5\\\" else fpm10)  # 公式(4)\\n                factors[p] = (ef, \\\"material_balance_formula4\\\", ef_unit)\\n        else:\\n            factors[p] = (float(d1[col]), \\\"table_D1_default\\\", ef_unit)\\n    if \\\"PM2.5\\\" in factors and d1_fuel in COAL_FUELS:\\n        ef25 = factors[\\\"PM2.5\\\"][0]\\n        factors[\\\"BC\\\"] = (ef25 * fbc, \\\"material_balance_formula5\\\", ef_unit)\\n        factors[\\\"OC\\\"] = (ef25 * foc, \\\"material_balance_formula6\\\", ef_unit)\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"Aar_ash_content\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"reported_input\\\", \\\"value\\\": ash_num, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"%\\\"})\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"ar_bottom_ash_ratio\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C1_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": ar, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"f_pm25\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C2_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": fpm25, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"f_pm10\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C2_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": fpm10, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"f_bc\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C3_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": fbc, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n        params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": \\\"f_oc\\\", \\\"mode\\\": \\\"table_C3_default\\\", \\\"value\\\": foc, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"fraction\\\"})\\n    elif d1_fuel in COAL_FUELS:\\n        problems.append((\\\"BC\\\", \\\"pm25_factor_unavailable\\\"))\\n        problems.append((\\\"OC\\\", \\\"pm25_factor_unavailable\\\"))\\n\\n    # 记录各污染物EF参数\\n    for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"CO\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]:\\n        if p in factors:\\n            ef, mode, u = factors[p]\\n            params.append({\\\"parameter_id\\\": f\\\"EF_{p}\\\", \\\"mode\\\": mode, \\\"value\\\": round(ef, 6), \\\"unit\\\": u})\\n\\n    return factors, params, problems\\n\\ndef build_components(sid, row, sector):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"构造燃料组件列表: [(comp_id, fuel_type, amount, unit, factors, params, problems)]\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    comps = []\\n    for comp, ftype_c, amt_c, unit_c in [\\n        (\\\"F1\\\", \\\"燃料一类型\\\", \\\"燃料一消耗量\\\", \\\"燃料一消耗量单位\\\"),\\n        (\\\"F2\\\", \\\"燃料二类型\\\", \\\"燃料二消耗量\\\", \\\"燃料二消耗量单位\\\"),\\n    ]:\\n        ftype = row.get(ftype_c)\\n        amt = row.get(amt_c)\\n        if pd.isna(ftype) or pd.isna(amt):\\n            continue\\n        try:\\n            amt = float(amt)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            continue\\n        if amt <= 0:\\n            continue\\n        unit = row.get(unit_c)\\n        unit = str(unit) if pd.notna(unit) else \\\"\\\"\\n        factors, params, problems = component_factors(sid, comp, str(ftype), amt, unit, sector, row)\\n        comps.append({\\\"comp\\\": comp, \\\"fuel\\\": str(ftype), \\\"amount\\\": amt, \\\"unit\\\": unit,\\n                      \\\"factors\\\": factors, \\\"params\\\": params, \\\"problems\\\": problems})\\n    # 其他燃料消耗总量(吨标准煤) 作为独立组件 F3\\n    oth = row.get(\\\"其他燃料消耗总量（吨标准煤）\\\")\\n    if pd.notna(oth):\\n        try:\\n            oth = float(oth)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            oth = None\\n        if oth is not None and oth > 0:\\n            # 若 F1 已以吨标准煤填报且数值相同(表单自动复填), 不重复\\n            dup = any(c[\\\"comp\\\"] == \\\"F1\\\" and c[\\\"unit\\\"] == \\\"吨标准煤\\\" and abs(c[\\\"amount\\\"] - oth) < 1e-9 for c in comps)\\n            if not dup:\\n                factors, params, problems = component_factors(sid, \\\"F3\\\", \\\"其他燃料\\\", oth, \\\"吨标准煤\\\", sector, row)\\n                comps.append({\\\"comp\\\": \\\"F3\\\", \\\"fuel\\\": \\\"其他燃料\\\", \\\"amount\\\": oth, \\\"unit\\\": \\\"吨标准煤\\\",\\n                              \\\"factors\\\": factors, \\\"params\\\": params, \\\"problems\\\": problems})\\n    return comps\\n\\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 5. 治理设施与控制效率 (表A.1 平均去除效率; 基101-2002 去除效率优先)\\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\\ndef match_measure(name):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"处理工艺名称 -> 表A.1 措施名 (关键词匹配)\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n = name\\n    # 脱硝\\n    if \\\"低氮燃烧\\\" in n and (\\\"选择性催化还原\\\" in n or \\\"SCR\\\" in n.replace(\\\"（\\\", \\\"(\\\")):\\n        return \\\"低氮燃烧技术+选择性催化还原法\\\"\\n    if \\\"低氮燃烧\\\" in n and (\\\"选择性非催化还原\\\" in n or \\\"SNCR\\\" in n):\\n        return \\\"低氮燃烧技术+选择性非催化还原法\\\"\\n    if \\\"选择性催化还原\\\" in n or \\\"SCR\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"选择性催化还原法\\\"\\n    if \\\"选择性非催化还原\\\" in n or \\\"SNCR\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"选择性非催化还原法\\\"\\n    # 脱硫\\n    if \\\"石灰石\\\" in n or \\\"石灰/石膏\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"石灰石/石膏法\\\"\\n    if \\\"双碱\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"双碱法\\\"\\n    if \\\"海水\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"海水法\\\"\\n    if \\\"氧化镁\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"氧化镁法\\\"\\n    if \\\"氨法\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"氨法\\\"\\n    if \\\"炉内喷钙\\\" in n or \\\"炉内脱硫\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"炉内喷钙法\\\"\\n    if \\\"烟气循环流化床\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"烟气循环流化床法\\\"\\n    if \\\"旋转喷雾干燥\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"旋转喷雾干燥法\\\"\\n    if \\\"密相干\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"密相干法\\\"\\n    # 除尘\\n    if \\\"电袋\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"电袋复合除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"静电\\\" in n or \\\"电除尘\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"高效静电除尘法\\\" if \\\"高效\\\" in n else \\\"普通静电除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"袋式\\\" in n or \\\"布袋\\\" in n or \\\"过滤除尘\\\" in n or \\\"滤芯\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"袋式除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"多管\\\" in n or \\\"旋风\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"多管旋风除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"重力沉降\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"重力沉降法\\\"\\n    if \\\"惯性\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"惯性除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"文丘里\\\" in n or \\\"水膜\\\" in n or \\\"湿式\\\" in n or \\\"湿法除尘\\\" in n or \\\"喷淋\\\" in n or \\\"水浴\\\" in n or \\\"水帘\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"湿式除尘法\\\"\\n    # VOC\\n    if \\\"蓄热式热力燃烧\\\" in n or \\\"蓄热式催化燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"蓄热式热力燃烧法\\\" if \\\"热力燃烧\\\" in n else \\\"催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"热力燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"热力燃烧\\\"\\n    if \\\"沸石转轮\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"沸石转轮吸附法\\\"\\n    if \\\"吸附/催化燃烧\\\" in n or \\\"吸附催化\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"吸附/催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"催化燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"活性炭\\\" in n or (\\\"吸附\\\" in n and \\\"蒸汽\\\" in n) or \\\"吸附\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"活性炭法\\\"\\n    if \\\"光催化\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"光催化\\\"\\n    if \\\"光解\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"光解\\\"\\n    if \\\"低温等离子体\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"低温等离子体\\\"\\n    if \\\"冷凝\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"冷凝法\\\"\\n    if \\\"生物过滤\\\" in n or \\\"生物滴滤\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"生物过滤法\\\"\\n    return None\\n\\n# 设施类型缺工艺名称时的缺省措施 (表A.1 \\\"其他X技术\\\")\\nFACILITY_TYPE_DEFAULT = {\\n    \\\"脱硫（酸）设施\\\": \\\"其他脱硫技术\\\",\\n    \\\"脱硝设施\\\": \\\"其他脱硝技术\\\",\\n    \\\"除尘设施\\\": \\\"其他除尘技术\\\",\\n    \\\"挥发性有机物治理设施\\\": None,   # 表A.1 无其他VOC技术默认值 -> 0\\n    \\\"其他废气污染物治理设施\\\": None,\\n}\\nPRIMARY_POLLUTANT = {\\n    \\\"脱硫（酸）设施\\\": [\\\"SO2\\\"],\\n    \\\"脱硝设施\\\": [\\\"NOx\\\"],\\n    \\\"除尘设施\\\": [\\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"],\\n    \\\"挥发性有机物治理设施\\\": [\\\"VOC\\\"],\\n    \\\"其他废气污染物治理设施\\\": [],\\n}\\nALL_POLLUTANT_KEYS = [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM2.5-10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]\\nA1_KEY = {\\\"SO2\\\": \\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\": \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\": \\\"VOCs\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\": \\\"PM25\\\",\\n          \\\"PM2.5-10\\\": \\\"PM2510\\\", \\\"BC\\\": \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\": \\\"OC\\\"}\\n\\n# 企业级: 电力热力源治理设施 (排放口名称=电站锅炉/燃气轮机排放口)\\ncf[\\\"_port_is_power\\\"] = cf[\\\"对应的排放口名称\\\"].astype(str) == \\\"电站锅炉/燃气轮机排放口\\\"\\ncf_by_ent = {}\\nfor _, r in cf.iterrows():\\n    cf_by_ent.setdefault(str(r[\\\"统一社会信用代码\\\"]), []).append(r)\\n\\ndef control_efficiencies(sid, row):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"返回 (eta, methods) — eta: {pollutant: 小数去除效率(0-1)}, methods: {pollutant: [措施名]}\\n       eta 包含 PM2.5 与 PM2.5-10 两组, 供 PM10 细/粗组分使用\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ent = str(row[\\\"统一社会信用代码\\\"])\\n    facilities = [r for r in cf_by_ent.get(ent, []) if r[\\\"_port_is_power\\\"]]\\n    eta = {p: 0.0 for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM2.5-10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]}\\n    methods = {p: [] for p in [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM2.5-10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]}\\n    measures_used = []\\n\\n    for r in facilities:\\n        ftype = str(r[\\\"废气治理设施名称\\\"]) if pd.notna(r[\\\"废气治理设施名称\\\"]) else \\\"\\\"\\n        proc = str(r[\\\"处理工艺名称\\\"]) if pd.notna(r[\\\"处理工艺名称\\\"]) else \\\"\\\"\\n        reported = r[\\\"去除效率（%）\\\"]\\n        rep = None\\n        try:\\n            rep = float(reported)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            rep = None\\n\\n        measure = match_measure(proc) if proc else None\\n        if measure is None:\\n            measure = FACILITY_TYPE_DEFAULT.get(ftype)\\n        if measure is None:\\n            continue\\n        if measure not in A1_BY_MEASURE:\\n            continue\\n        row_a1 = A1_BY_MEASURE[measure]\\n        primary = PRIMARY_POLLUTANT.get(ftype, [])\\n        for pk in ALL_POLLUTANT_KEYS:\\n            val = float(row_a1[A1_KEY[pk]]) / 100.0\\n            if pk in primary and rep is not None:\\n                val = rep / 100.0\\n            if val > eta[pk]:\\n                # 该措施对该污染物有效时记录; 同一污染物多措施按串联合并(1-(1-a)(1-b))\\n                if val > 0 and measure not in methods[pk]:\\n                    methods[pk].append(measure)\\n                    measures_used.append((pk, measure, val))\\n    # 低氮燃烧技术(基102 行字段) — 若治理设施措施中已含低氮燃烧则不重复\\n    low_nox_flag = str(row.get(\\\"是否采用低氮燃烧技术\\\")) if pd.notna(row.get(\\\"是否采用低氮燃烧技术\\\")) else \\\"否\\\"\\n    has_low_nox_measure = any(\\\"低氮燃烧\\\" in m for m in methods[\\\"NOx\\\"])\\n    if low_nox_flag == \\\"是\\\" and not has_low_nox_measure:\\n        methods[\\\"NOx\\\"].append(\\\"低氮燃烧技术\\\")\\n        measures_used.append((\\\"NOx\\\", \\\"低氮燃烧技术\\\", 0.22))\\n    elif low_nox_flag == \\\"是\\\" and has_low_nox_measure:\\n        measures_used.append((\\\"NOx\\\", \\\"低氮燃烧技术(已含于组合措施)\\\", 0.0))\\n\\n    # 串联合并\\n    for pk, items in methods.items():\\n        comb = 1.0\\n        for _, m, v in measures_used:\\n            if m in items:\\n                comb *= (1.0 - v)\\n        eta[pk] = 1.0 - comb\\n    return eta, methods\\n\\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6. 主流程: 逐源核算\\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\\nitem_rows = []          # generic_inventory_items.csv 行\\ndecision_rows = []      # source_decisions.csv 行\\ndisposition_rows = []   # generic_source_disposition.csv 行\\nexceptions = []         # exception 组\\n\\nreason_codes = {\\n    \\\"POWER\\\": \\\"b102_power_device\\\",\\n    \\\"BOILER\\\": \\\"b102_industrial_boiler_device\\\",\\n    \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\": \\\"waste_heat_boiler_no_combustion\\\",\\n}\\nITEM_REASON = {\\n    \\\"calculated\\\": \\\"all_components_factor_available\\\",\\n    \\\"not_involved_nh3\\\": \\\"standard_no_nh3_factor_for_power\\\",\\n    \\\"not_involved_target\\\": \\\"not_in_target_scope\\\",\\n    \\\"not_involved_out\\\": \\\"source_out_of_scope\\\",\\n}\\n\\ndef fmt(x):\\n    if x is None:\\n        return \\\"\\\"\\n    return f\\\"{x:.6f}\\\".rstrip(\\\"0\\\").rstrip(\\\".\\\") if isinstance(x, float) else str(x)\\n\\nfor sid in MANIFEST[\\\"candidate_ids\\\"]:\\n    info = DECIDED[sid]\\n    row = info[\\\"row\\\"]\\n    decided = info[\\\"decided\\\"]\\n    ind_code = row[\\\"行业类别代码\\\"]\\n\\n    # ---- 决策输出 ----\\n    if decided == \\\"POWER\\\":\\n        disposition = \\\"calculated\\\"  # 占位, 下面按核算结果修正\\n        sector = sector_of(ind_code)\\n    elif decided == \\\"BOILER\\\":\\n        disposition = \\\"not_in_target_scope\\\"\\n    else:\\n        disposition = \\\"out_of_scope\\\"\\n\\n    if decided == \\\"POWER\\\":\\n        sector = sector_of(ind_code)\\n        comps = 1e3 / 1e6 * (ef25 * (1 - eta[\\\"PM2.5\\\"]) + (ef10 - ef25) * (1 - eta[\\\"PM2.5-10\\\"]))\\n                    item_em[p] = em\\n                else:\\n                    key = p if p !=   return \\\"氧化镁法\\\"\\n    if \\\"氨法\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"氨法\\\"\\n    if \\\"炉内喷钙\\\" in n or \\\"炉内脱硫\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"炉内喷钙法\\\"\\n    if \\\"烟气循环流化床\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"烟气循环流化床法\\\"\\n    if \\\"半干法\\\" in n or \\\"旋转喷雾干燥\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"旋转喷雾干燥法\\\"\\n    if \\\"密相干\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"密相干法\\\"\\n    if \\\"湿法脱硫\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"石灰石/石膏法\\\"   # 湿法脱硫(一体化)按石灰石/石膏法近似(表A.1主导湿法脱硫措施)\\n    if \\\"电袋\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"电袋复合除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"湿式电除尘\\\" in n or \\\"湿电\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"高效静电除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"静电\\\" in n or \\\"电除尘\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"高效静电除尘法\\\" if \\\"高效\\\" in n else \\\"普通静电除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"袋式\\\" in n or \\\"布袋\\\" in n or \\\"过滤除尘\\\" in n or \\\"滤芯\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"袋式除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"多管\\\" in n or \\\"旋风\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"多管旋风除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"重力沉降\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"重力沉降法\\\"\\n    if \\\"惯性\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"惯性除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"文丘里\\\" in n or \\\"水膜\\\" in n or \\\"湿式\\\" in n or \\\"湿法\\\" in n or \\\"喷淋\\\" in n or \\\"水浴\\\" in n or \\\"水帘\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"湿式除尘法\\\"\\n    if \\\"蓄热式热力燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"蓄热式热力燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"蓄热式催化燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"热力燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"热力燃烧\\\"\\n    if \\\"沸石转轮\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"沸石转轮吸附法\\\"\\n    if \\\"吸附/催化燃烧\\\" in n or \\\"吸附催化\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"吸附/催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"催化燃烧\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"催化燃烧法\\\"\\n    if \\\"活性炭\\\" in n or \\\"吸附\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"活性炭法\\\"\\n    if \\\"光催化\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"光催化\\\"\\n    if \\\"光解\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"光解\\\"\\n    if \\\"低温等离子体\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"低温等离子体\\\"\\n    if \\\"冷凝\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"冷凝法\\\"\\n    if \\\"生物过滤\\\" in n or \\\"生物滴滤\\\" in n:\\n        return \\\"生物过滤法\\\"\\n    return None\\n\\nFACILITY_TYPE_DEFAULT = {\\n    \\\"脱硫（酸）设施\\\": \\\"其他脱硫技术\\\",\\n    \\\"脱硝设施\\\": \\\"其他脱硝技术\\\",\\n    \\\"除尘设施\\\": \\\"其他除尘技术\\\",\\n    \\\"挥发性有机物治理设施\\\": None,\\n    \\\"其他废气污染物治理设施\\\": None,\\n}\\nPRIMARY_POLLUTANT = {\\n    \\\"脱硫（酸）设施\\\": [\\\"SO2\\\"],\\n    \\\"脱硝设施\\\": [\\\"NOx\\\"],\\n    \\\"除尘设施\\\": [\\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM2.5-10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"],\\n    \\\"挥发性有机物治理设施\\\": [\\\"VOC\\\"],\\n    \\\"其他废气污染物治理设施\\\": [],\\n}\\nA1_KEY = {\\\"SO2\\\": \\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\": \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\": \\\"VOCs\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\": \\\"PM25\\\",\\n          \\\"PM2.5-10\\\": \\\"PM2510\\\", \\\"BC\\\": \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\": \\\"OC\\\"}\\nETA_KEYS = [\\\"SO2\\\", \\\"NOx\\\", \\\"VOC\\\", \\\"PM2.5\\\", \\\"PM2.5-10\\\", \\\"BC\\\", \\\"OC\\\"]\\n\\ncf[\\\"_port_is_power\\\"] = cf[\\\"对应的排放口名称\\\"].astype(str) == \\\"电站锅炉/燃气轮机排放口\\\"\\ncf_by_ent = {}\\nfor _, r in cf.iterrows():\\n    cf_by_ent.setdefault(str(r[\\\"统一社会信用代码\\\"]), []).append(r)\\n\\ndef control_efficiencies(row):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"返回 (eta, methods)\\n       eta: {pollutant: 去除效率(0~1)}, 含 PM2.5 与 PM2.5-10\\n       methods: {pollutant: [措施名]}\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    facilities = [r for r in cf_by_ent.get(str(row[\\\"统一社会信用代码\\\"]), []) if r[\\\"_port_is_power\\\"]]\\n    # (pollutant, measure) -> 效率最大值; 相同措施去重, 多措施串联合并\\n    meas_eff = {k: {} for k in ETA_KEYS}\\n    for r in facilities:\\n        ftype = str(r[\\\"废气治理设施名称\\\"]) if pd.notna(r[\\\"废气治理设施名称\\\"]) else \\\"\\\"\\n        proc = str(r[\\\"处理工艺名称\\\"]) if pd.notna(r[\\\"处理工艺名称\\\"]) else \\\"\\\"\\n        rep = None\\n        try:\\n            rep = float(r[\\\"去除效率（%）\\\"])\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            rep = None\\n        measure = match_measure(proc) if proc else None\\n        if measure is None:\\n            measure = FACILITY_TYPE_DEFAULT.get(ftype)\\n        if measure is None or measure not in A1_BY_MEASURE:\\n            continue\\n        measures = [measure]\\n        if measure == \\\"SNCR+SCR\\\":\\n            measures = [\\\"选择性非催化还原法\\\", \\\"选择性催化还原法\\\"]\\n        primary = PRIMARY_POLLUTANT.get(ftype, [])\\n        for m in measures:\\n            a1 = A1_BY_MEASURE[m]\\n            for pk in ETA_KEYS:\\n                val = float(a1[A1_KEY[pk]]) / 100.0\\n                if pk in primary and rep is not None:\\n                    val = rep / 100.0\\n                if val > 0:\\n                    meas_eff[pk].setdefault(m, 0.0)\\n                    meas_eff[pk][m] = max(meas_eff[pk][m], val)\\n    # 低氮燃烧技术(基102行字段) —— 若治理设施措施已含\\\"低氮燃烧\\\"则不再重复叠加\\n    low_nox_flag = str(row.get(\\\"是否采用低氮燃烧技术\\\")) if pd.notna(row.get(\\\"是否采用低氮燃烧技术\\\")) else \\\"否\\\"\\n    if low_nox_flag == \\\"是\\\" and not any(\\\"低氮燃烧\\\" in m for m in meas_eff[\\\"NOx\\\"]):\\n        meas_eff[\\\"NOx\\\"].setdefault(\\\"低氮燃烧技术\\\", 0.0)\\n        meas_eff[\\\"NOx\\\"][\\\"低氮燃烧技术\\\"] = max(meas_eff[\\\"NOx\\\"][\\\"低氮燃烧技术\\\"], 0.22)\\n    eta = {}\\n    methods = {}\\n    for pk in ETA_KEYS:\\n        comb = 1.0\\n        for m, v in meas_eff[pk].items():\\n            comb *= (1.0 - v)\\n        eta[pk] = 1.0 - comb\\n        methods[pk] = list(meas_eff[pk].keys())\\n    return eta, methods\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6. 逐源核算\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\ndef fmt(x):\\n    if x is None:\\n        return \\\"\\\"\\n    return f\\\"{x:.6f}\\\".rstrip(\\\"0\\\").rstrip(\\\".\\\") if isinstance(x, float) else str(x)\\n\\ndef calc_method_str(status, p, comps):\\n    if status != \\\"calculated\\\":\\n        return \\\"N/A\\\"\\n    modes = set()\\n    for c in comps:\\n        if p in c[\\\"factors\\\"]:\\n            modes.add(c[\\\"factors\\\"][p][1])\\n    if \\\"material_balance_formula3\\\" in modes:\\n        return \\\"物料衡算法(公式3)+产排污系数法(公式2)\\\"\\n    if modes & {\\\"material_balance_formula4\\\", \\\"material_balance_formula5\\\", \\\"material_balance_formula6\\\"}:\\n        return \\\"物料衡算法(公式4~6)+产排污系数法(公式2)\\\"\\n    return \\\"产排污系数法(公式2), EF取自表D.1\\\"\\n\\ndef standard_ref(status):\\n    if status == \\\"calculated\\\":\\n        return \\\"T/CSES 144-2024 7.2 公式(2), 附录D 表D.1, 附录A 表A.1\\\"\\n    if status == \\\"not_involved\\\":\\n        return \\\"T/CSES 144-2024 第7章电力热力源核算范围\\\"\\n    return \\\"T/CSES 144-2024 附录D 表D.1(无对应产生系数)\\\"\\n\\ndef reason_for_item(status, p, comps):\\n    if status == \\\"calculated\\\":\\n        return \\\"all_components_factor_available\\\"\\n    if status == \\\"not_involved\\\":\\n        return \\\"standard_no_nh3_factor_for_power\\\" if p == \\\"NH3\\\" else (\\n            \\\"source_out_of_scope\\\" if comps is None else \\\"not_in_target_scope\\\")\\n    reasons = set()\\n    for c in comps:\\n        for pp, rc in c[\\\"problems\\\"]:\\n            if pp == p:\\n                reasons.add(rc)\\n    return \\\",\\\".join(sorted(reasons)) if reasons else \\\"factor_unavailable\\\"\\n\\ndecision_rows, disposition_rows, item_rows = [], [], []\\n\\nfor sid in MANIFEST[\\\"candidate_ids\\\"]:\\n    iloc = sid_to_iloc[sid]\\n    row = b102.iloc[iloc]\\n    dev_type = None\\n    if pd.notna(row[\\\"电站锅炉/燃气轮机编号\\\"]):\\n        dev_type = str(row[\\\"电站锅炉/燃气轮机类型\\\"])\\n        decided = \\\"POWER\\\"\\n    elif pd.notna(row[\\\"工业锅炉编号\\\"]):\\n        dev_type = str(row[\\\"工业锅炉类型\\\"])\\n        decided = \\\"BOILER\\\"\\n    else:\\n        decided = \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\"\\n    if dev_type == \\\"余热利用锅炉\\\":\\n        decided = \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\"\\n\\n    reason_decision = {\\\"POWER\\\": \\\"b102_power_device\\\", \\\"BOILER\\\": \\\"b102_industrial_boiler_device\\\",\\n                       \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\": \\\"waste_heat_boiler_no_combustion\\\"}[decided]\\n\\n    if decided == \\\"POWER\\\":\\n        sector = sector_of(row[\\\"行业类别代码\\\"])\\n        comps = build_components(row, sector)\\n        eta, methods = control_efficiencies(row)\\n\\n        item_status, item_gen, item_em, item_missing = {}, {}, {}, {}\\n        for p in POLLUTANTS:\\n            if p == \\\"NH3\\\":\\n                item_status[p], item_gen[p], item_em[p] = \\\"not_involved\\\", None, None\\n                continue\\n            missing = [c[\\\"comp\\\"] for c in comps if p not in c[\\\"factors\\\"]]\\n            item_missing[p] = missing\\n            if comps and not missing:\\n                gen = 0.0\\n                for c in comps:\\n                    ef, mode, u = c[\\\"factors\\\"][p]\\n                    gen += (c[\\\"amount\\\"] * 1e4 * ef / 1e6) if u == \\\"g/m3\\\" else (c[\\\"amount\\\"] * 1e3 * ef / 1e6)\\n                item_status[p], item_gen[p] = \\\"calculated\\\", gen\\n                if p == \\\"PM10\\\":\\n                    em = 0.0\\n                    for c in comps:\\n                        ef25, ef10 = c[\\\"factors\\\"][\\\"PM2.5\\\"][0], c[\\\"factors\\\"][\\\"PM10\\\"][0]\\n                        u = c[\\\"factors\\\"][\\\"PM10\\\"][2]\\n                        em25 = ef25 * (1 - eta[\\\"PM2.5\\\"])\\n                        emcoarse = (ef10 - ef25) * (1 - eta[\\\"PM2.5-10\\\"])\\n                        em += (c[\\\"amount\\\"] * 1e4 / 1e6) * (em25 + emcoarse) if u == \\\"g/m3\\\" else (c[\\\"amount\\\"] * 1e3 / 1e6) * (em25 + emcoarse)\\n                    item_em[p] = em\\n                else:\\n                    key = p if p != \\\"VOC\\\" else \\\"VOC\\\"\\n                    item_em[p] = gen * (1 - eta[key])\\n            else:\\n                item_status[p], item_gen[p], item_em[p] = \\\"information_insufficient\\\", None, None\\n\\n        disposition = \\\"calculated\\\" if any(s == \\\"calculated\\\" for s in item_status.values()) else \\\"information_insufficient\\\"\\n\\n        for p in POLLUTANTS:\\n            act_records, par_records, ctl_records = [], [], []\\n            for c in comps:\\n                act_records.append({\\\"component_id\\\": c[\\\"comp\\\"], \\\"value\\\": c[\\\"amount\\\"], \\\"unit\\\": c[\\\"unit\\\"]})\\n                for pr in c[\\\"params\\\"]:\\n                    rec = {\\\"component_id\\\": c[\\\"comp\\\"]}\\n                    rec.update(pr)\\n                    par_records.append(rec)\\n                if p == \\\"PM10\\\":\\n                    ms = list(dict.fromkeys(methods[\\\"PM2.5\\\"] + methods[\\\"PM2.5-10\\\"]))\\n                    ctl_records.append({\\\"component_id\\\": c[\\\"comp\\\"],\\n                                        \\\"method\\\": \\\";\\\".join(ms) if ms else \\\"none\\\",\\n                                        \\\"efficiency\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"fine_efficiency\\\": fmt(eta[\\\"PM2.5\\\"]),\\n                                        \\\"coarse_efficiency\\\": fmt(eta[\\\"PM2.5-10\\\"])})\\n                elif p == \\\"NH3\\\":\\n                    ctl_records.append({\\\"component_id\\\": c[\\\"comp\\\"], \\\"method\\\": \\\"none\\\", \\\"efficiency\\\": 0,\\n                                        \\\"fine_efficiency\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"coarse_efficiency\\\": \\\"\\\"})\\n                else:\\n                    key = p if p != \\\"VOC\\\" else \\\"VOC\\\"\\n                    eff = eta.get(key, 0.0)\\n                    ms = methods.get(key, [])\\n                    ctl_records.append({\\\"component_id\\\": c[\\\"comp\\\"], \\\"method\\\": \\\";\\\".join(ms) if ms else \\\"none\\\",\\n                                        \\\"efficiency\\\": fmt(eff), \\\"fine_efficiency\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"coarse_efficiency\\\": \\\"\\\"})\\n            status = item_status[p]\\n            item_rows.append({\\n                \\\"source_id\\\": sid, \\\"target\\\": decided, \\\"pollutant\\\": p, \\\"status\\\": status,\\n                \\\"method\\\": calc_method_str(status, p, comps),\\n                \\\"activity_record_json\\\": json.dumps(act_records, ensure_ascii=False),\\n                \\\"parameter_record_json\\\": json.dumps(par_records, ensure_ascii=False),\\n  \\\"standard_reference\\\": standard_ref(status),\\n                \\\"reason_code\\\": reason_for_item(status, p, comps if status == \\\"information_insufficient\\\" else None),\\n            })\\n    else:\\n        for p in POLLUTANTS:\\n            item_rows.append({\\n                \\\"source_id\\\": sid, \\\"target\\\": decided, \\\"pollutant\\\": p, \\\"status\\\": \\\"not_involved\\\",\\n                \\\"method\\\": \\\"N/A\\\",\\n                \\\"activity_record_json\\\": \\\"[]\\\", \\\"parameter_record_json\\\": \\\"[]\\\", \\\"control_record_json\\\": \\\"[]\\\",\\n                \\\"generation_t\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"emission_t\\\": \\\"\\\",\\n                \\\"standard_reference\\\": \\\"T/CSES 144-2024 第7章电力热力源核算范围\\\",\\n                \\\"reason_code\\\": \\\"source_out_of_scope\\\" if decided == \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\" else \\\"not_in_target_scope\\\",\\n            })\\n        disposition = \\\"out_of_scope\\\" if decided == \\\"OUT_OF_SCOPE\\\" else \\\"not_in_target_scope\\\"\\n\\n    decision_rows.append({\\\"source_id\\\": sid, \\\"requested_target\\\": MANIFEST[\\\"target\\\"],\\n return next(sid for sid in MANIFEST[\\\"candidate_ids\\\"] if DECIDED_BY_SID[sid] == \\\"POWER\\\")\\n\\nDECIDED_BY_SID = {r[\\\"source_id\\\"]: r[\\\"decided_target\\\"] for r in decision_rows}\\nmsw_sids, bio_sids, waste_sids, whb_sids = [], [], [], []\\nfor sid in MANIFEST[\\\"candidate_ids\\\"]:\\n    row = b102.iloc[sid_to_iloc[sid]]\\n    if DECIDED_BY_SID[sid] ==   whb_sids.append(sid)\\n\\nEX = []\\ndef add_exc(eid, sids, pollutant, root_cause, action):\\n    EX.append({\\\"exception_id\\\": eid, \\\"source_id\\\": sids[0], \\\"pollutant\\\": pollutant,\\n 或确认排除\\\")\\nadd_exc(\\\"EX-03\\\", waste_sids, \\\"SO2,NOx,CO,VOC,PM2.5,PM10,BC,OC\\\",\\n        \\\"工业废料(用于燃料): 表D.1无对应燃料产生系数\\\",\\n        \\\"由用户确认燃料属性并补充系数, 实际完成的工作
1. 解析运行包输入
   - run_manifest.json 指向 6 个输入：基102-2002（4442 行设备燃料表）、基101-2001 四片企业表（共 3022 行）、基101-2002（8067 行治理设施表）、source_identity_index.json（4442 条 source_id↔基102行映射，与 manifest candidate_ids 完全一致）。
   - T_CSES_144-2024.pdf（86 页）全文提取为 scratch_pdf_text.txt，并按版面顺序解析出参数表：
     scratch_table_d1.json（表D.1 电力热力源产生系数 70 行）、scratch_table_c1/c2/c3.json（表C.1~C.3 燃煤参数）、scratch_table_a1.json（表A.1 控制措施平均去除效率 74 项），已做关键行抽查核对。
2. 源分类（依据基102 原始字段，未用任何外部答案）
   - 电站锅炉/燃气轮机编号非空 = POWER（561 行，其中余热利用锅炉 8 行转范围外）；
   - 工业锅炉编号非空 = BOILER（3881 行）；设备类型=余热利用锅炉 = OUT_OF_SCOPE（共 21 行）。
3. 核算逻辑（依据标准公式）
   - 产排污系数法 E=A×EF×(1−η)（公式2）；
   - 燃煤 SO2/PM2.5/PM10/BC/OC 按物料衡算公式(3)~(6)，S 与灰分以 g/kg 代入、气体燃料系数按表D.1注c 取 g/m3；
   - NOx 燃煤按装机容量分档（≤100MW 8.96；(100,300)MW 8.19；≥300MW 7.21）；
   - 行业映射：4411/4412/4417/4419→电力生产、4430→热力生产和供应、其余（自备电厂/制造企业）→电力供应（与表E.7一致并已在异常清单记录）；
   - 治理效率：企业基101-2002 中"电站锅炉/燃气轮机排放口"设施，去除效率(%)优先，缺省用表A.1（按设施类型缺省：其他脱硫40%/其他脱硝20%/其他除尘10%），多措施串联合并，低氮燃烧22%，投运率因无运行时间数据按 1.0 假设（已在 EX-07 记录）。
4. 运行结果（build_inventory.py 已执行完成，输出已写入 outputs/）
   - outputs/source_decisions.csv：4442 行
   - outputs/generic_inventory_items.csv：39978 行（4442源×9污染物）
   - outputs/generic_source_disposition.csv：4442 行
   - outputs/generic_exception_list.csv：9 个根因组（EX-01~EX-09）
   - outputs/generic_run_summary.json：含输入文件、计数、状态统计、规则与脚本路径、未完成事项
   - 状态计数：calculated 2472、information_insufficient 1952、not_involved 35554（决策计数：POWER 553、BOILER 3868、OUT_OF_SCOPE 21）。
